# Skills · R · part 93

852 entries, review-dashboard to review-fix. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-dashboard**: プロジェクトを区分に分割し、GitHub Issue のダッシュボードで管理して、実行1回につき1区分だけを多観点でレビューするスキル。1実行1区分で完結するため cron・スケジュール実行にも適する。起動条件: `/review-dashboard` の実行、スキル名の明示的な指定、スケジュール実行からの呼び出しのみ。レビュー依頼一般では自動起動しない。単一テーマの全体監査は architecture-audit / implement-audit / spec-sync、区分の多観点深掘りは本スキル。 ([tuanemuy/dotfiles](https://github.com/tuanemuy/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/review-dashboard))

- **review-fix**: Applies review:review-comments decisions by delegating each FIX or FIX-STYLE item to its own subagent, then runs auto-detected verification. ([shoto290/shoto](https://github.com/shoto290/shoto/tree/HEAD/plugins/review/skills/review-fix) · ★2)
- **review-fix**: Address the unresolved review threads on a PR, the given number or the current branch's. Triages the threads, has a subagent apply the fixes and prove mutation fixes, removes narrating comments with h2xd:review-code-comments, then commits, pushes, replies on each thread and resolves the fulfilled ones, without asking while the checks pass. Use after a review, from review-pr or a person. ([h2xd/skills](https://github.com/h2xd/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-fix) · ★1)
